TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在评估城市社区安全时，是否追踪真实犯罪风险还是受社区人口统计刻板印象影响。通过分离"名称"与"地理坐标"的实验设计，发现七个模型中大多数仅依赖名称信息，且名称对安全评级的影响与当地边缘化群体比例相关，表明存在人口统计刻板印象。这种效应在控制犯罪和收入后依然存在，且随模型地理知识增强而放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于城市安全决策（如步行、租房、旅行地点选择），然而这些模型的安全判断可能受到社区名称所附带的人口统计刻板印象影响，而非真实风险。需要系统评估这种偏差是否存在及其机制。

Method: 对七个指令微调模型进行探测，设计三种条件（仅坐标、仅名称、名称+坐标）以分离名称与地理信息，覆盖洛杉矶和芝加哥186个社区，将评级与暴力犯罪数据及美国社区调查数据关联分析。

Result: （1）六个模型在仅坐标条件下评级几乎平坦，名称承载了大部分社区间差异且与暴力犯罪中等程度校准；（2）名称降低了边缘化群体比例较高社区的安全评级，该效应在两个城市七个模型中均存在，在洛杉矶控制犯罪和收入后仍显著；（3）过度谨慎追踪的是充分报告的凶杀案而非执行驱动型犯罪；（4）地理知识更强的模型应用更多人口统计刻板印象。

Conclusion: 社区名称同时包含真实犯罪信号和人口统计刻板印象，移除名称虽能减少偏见但也会降低准确性。这对LLM在建议和决策支持场景的部署具有重要启示，需要在偏见与准确之间谨慎权衡。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to inform safety decisions in cities, such as where it is safe to walk, rent, or travel. We ask whether such judgments track measured risk or the patterns attached to an urban neighborhood's name. We probe seven instruct-tuned models under three conditions that dissociate name from geography: coordinates-only, name-only, and name+coordinates, across 186 neighborhoods in Los Angeles and Chicago, joined to violent crime and American Community Survey data. First, ratings are nearly flat under coordinates for six of seven models, while names carry most between neighborhood variation and are moderately calibrated to violent crime; only at frontier scale does the coordinate channel show appreciable variation. Second, names lower safety ratings more for neighborhoods with higher shares of the locally dominant marginalized group (percent Black in Chicago, percent Hispanic in Los Angeles), and this name effect tracks demographic share in all seven models and both cities. In Los Angeles, where demographic share and crime are more separable, the effect survives controls for crime and income and is confirmed by crime-matched pairs. An enforcement-elasticity analysis further shows that over-caution tracks near-fully-reported homicide rather than discretionary, deployment-driven offenses. Third, the effect scales with geographic knowledge: models that better distinguish real neighborhoods apply more demographic stereotype to them. Because neighborhood names carry both genuine crime signal and demographic stereotype, removing names reduces both bias and accuracy. We discuss implications for deploying LLMs in advice and decision-support settings.

</details>


### [249] [Invocation-Level Reliability of Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26189)
*Afiya Noorain,Subhranshu Mohanty,Amritesh Banerjee,Abhijit Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究工具使用智能体的错误传播问题，发现其失败源于工具选择错误或参数参数错误，并揭示了一个测量学缺陷：在精确匹配评分下，错误传播模型的严重性和恢复参数被评分规则固定，无法真实估计。作者提出条件状态评分方案，可以对缓存完成进行零成本的回顾性修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用智能体在早期步骤的一个小错误会连锁破坏后续所有推理，但现有的精确匹配评分方法无法正确衡量这种错误传播，导致模型能力评估失真。

Method: 在五个开放权重模型上，于无污染的多步骤任务（深度1-8）上分别测量教师强制上下文和模型自由运行上下文下的正确调用率。通过分析精确匹配评分规则下后发散状态的目标值生成机制，推导出严重度和恢复参数被评分规则固定的理论结果，并提出条件状态评分方案作为补救。

Result: 深度6时，模型约70%的干净上下文能力被自身早期错误抵消（L6=0.686, 0.684）。在精确匹配评分下，869个中毒步骤0%正确（严重度参数被迫到边界），580个中毒步骤的恢复率结构性不可观测（期望随机概率仅0.0058）。拟合得到的0.92和0.73与实际值1.000严重不符。条件状态评分使严重度估算从中毒边界恢复到内部估计（+0.149, +0.316）。

Conclusion: 精确匹配评分规则本身决定了错误传播模型参数的"估计结果"，而非数据所揭示。条件状态评分可以在零额外成本下对已缓存的完成结果进行回顾性修复，获得真实的参数估计。

Abstract: Tool-using agents fail two ways: choosing the wrong tool, or forming wrong arguments, and an early failure of either kind can silently corrupt everything downstream. We measure a correct-invocation rate that separates the two, under both a clean teacher-forced context and the model's own free-running context, on five open-weight models over contamination-free multi-step tasks (depths 1-8). By depth 6, roughly 70% of a model's own clean-context capability is lost to its own earlier mistakes (L6 = 0.686, 0.684). Our central finding concerns the measurement itself. Under exact-match scoring against a fixed gold trajectory, a propagation model's severity and recovery parameters are not merely hard to estimate - they are fixed by the scoring rule. Severity is forced to its boundary (0 of 869 poisoned steps correct); recovery is structurally unobservable (0 of 580 poisoned steps returned on-track, against an expected 0.0058 by chance). Both follow from one mechanism: post-divergence, the gold value is generated by tool constants the model never sees, so it is information the model cannot derive. A fit run anyway returns 0.92 and 0.73 for a quantity that is exactly 1.000 - confident numbers for a parameter the scoring rule already determined. We give the mechanism and a remedy, conditional-on-state scoring, applied retrospectively to cached completions at zero additional cost, which un-pins severity to interior estimates excluding zero (+0.149, +0.316).

</details>


### [250] [Predicting Consequences and Reinforcing Navigation Policies with Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26190)
*Zengmao Wang,Wei Gao,Shuhan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于机器人导航的兼容性预测潜在世界模型（LWM），通过在潜在空间中预测动作条件下的特征兼容性来替代传统观测重建，从而简化世界模型并提升决策效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型主要关注重建未来观测或特征，引入了不必要的复杂性，限制了其在决策中的有效性。作者认为空间邻近性与潜在特征相似性相关，可以在潜在空间中直接评估动作后果。

Method: LWM预测动作条件下的潜在特征兼容性而非重建观测；利用跨轨迹采样的动作序列进行反事实训练；学习预测哪些动作序列更接近目标；通过无标注视频数据监督策略学习，并完全在世界模型内进行强化学习。

Result: 在多个真实世界机器人导航数据集上的实验表明，该方法在预测准确性、策略学习和真实世界导航性能上显著优于先前的世界模型和模仿学习方法。

Conclusion: 该想象驱动的框架无需动作标注和额外环境交互，即可有效实现机器人导航策略学习，验证了潜在空间兼容性预测作为世界模型范式的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: World models enable agents to reason about future outcomes and learn policies from their knowledge of state transition, but existing approaches primarily focus on reconstructing future observations or features, which introduces unnecessary complexity and limits their effectiveness for decision making. In this work, we propose a compatibility prediction Latent World Model (LWM) for robot navigation that predicts action-conditioned latent feature compatibility rather than reconstructing observations. Our key insight is that spatial proximity correlates with latent feature similarity, enabling action consequences to be evaluated directly in latent space. To support counterfactual training, our model leverages action sequences sampled across trajectories and learns to predict which sequences lead closer to the goal. Furthermore, we demonstrate how the learned world model can supervise policy learning from unlabeled video data and further improve policies through reinforcement learning entirely within the world model. This imagination-driven framework eliminates the need for action annotations and additional environment interaction. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world robot navigation datasets show that our approach significantly outperforms prior world model and imitation learning methods in prediction accuracy, policy learning, and real-world navigation performance. The code, pretrained models, and additional materials are available at https://wzm206.github.io/latent-world-model-nav.

</details>


### [251] [Structured Evidence Routing for Incident Risk Prediction from Multimodal Longitudinal EHRs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26191)
*Animesh Agarwal,Meysam Ghaffari,Nina Fatehi,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"结构化证据路由"（structured evidence routing）方法，通过路由器-预测器-审阅者（router-predictor-reviewer）工作流来从纵向电子健康记录（EHR）中预测疾病发病风险。该方法将完整的病史访问与疾病特定评估分离，在五个1年发病诊断任务上达到与监督式EHRSHOT基线相当的AUROC和AUPRC性能，同时提供了患者特定的证据链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向EHR中的发病风险预测面临挑战：相关信号是多模态的、单独来看较微弱、且分布在不规则的患者历史记录中，难以有效提取和利用。

Method: 提出路由器-预测器-审阅者（router-predictor-reviewer）三段式工作流。路由器将完整的索引前EHR组织为紧凑摘要和针对性证据切片；预测器利用这些证据形成证据关联的风险评估；审阅者对该评估进行批判性审查。同时将路由证据摘要与有监督分类器读出相配对，以便与监督式EHRSHOT基线比较。内部还进行了预读出消融实验。

Result: 在五个1年发病诊断任务上，该方法达到已建立的监督式EHRSHOT基线的AUROC范围，并在AUPRC上保持竞争力，同时提供了患者特定的证据链。内部消融实验表明Routing（路由）、实验室证据、任务引导和审阅环节均对性能有贡献。

Conclusion: 结构化证据路由方法能够在保持与有监督基线相当预测性能的同时，提供可解释的患者特定证据链，且各模块（路由、证据、引导、审阅）均对性能有积极贡献。

Abstract: Incident risk prediction from longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs) is challenging because relevant signals are multimodal, weak in isolation, and distributed across irregular patient histories. We propose structured evidence routing, a router-predictor-reviewer workflow that separates full-record access from disease-specific assessment. The router organizes the complete pre-index EHR into a compact summary and targeted evidence slices; the predictor uses this evidence to form an evidence-linked risk assessment, which the reviewer critiques. For comparison with supervised EHRSHOT baselines, we pair the routed evidence summaries with a supervised classifier readout. Across five 1-year incident diagnosis tasks, our method reaches the AUROC range of established supervised EHRSHOT baselines and remains competitive on AUPRC, while exposing a patient-specific evidence trail. Internal pre-readout ablations further suggest that routing, laboratory evidence, task guidance, and review each contribute to performance.

</details>


### [252] [AffectOmni: RL-Verifiable People-Centric Grounded Affective Reasoning for Social and Art-Related Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26193)
*Yibo Wang,Rui Yang,Jisheng Dang,Bimei Wang,Yitao Wu,Pengfei Cao,Wencan Zhang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AffectOmni，一种基于GRPO训练的框架，用于增强多模态大语言模型的情感推理能力。通过引入People Focus和Temporal Order奖励机制，并采用组内比较评分策略，解决模型在情感推理中忽视人物线索和评分聚类的问题，在多个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在VQA和场景理解上表现优异，但情感推理中容易走捷径——模型可能忽视微表情、肢体语言等人物中心线索而出错，导致可追溯性和外部验证性差。现有RL方法主要奖励上下文或逻辑连贯性，未显式强化对人物证据的注意力；同时LLM作为评审的打分常出现分数聚类问题，降低奖励区分度。

Method: AffectOmni采用GRPO训练框架，设计了两项核心奖励：People Focus奖励鼓励模型关注人物为主的证据选择，Temporal Order奖励促进时序结构化推理；同时采用组内比较评分（within-group comparative scoring）产生更稳定且具区分度的奖励信号。在验证环节，Thinking Summarizer将自由形式推理转化为可执行证据指令，并通过SAM3将证据锚定到像素级区域，提供训练循环外部的可审计接口。

Result: 在IntentBench、Daily Omni和WorldSense基准上的实验显示，AffectOmni相对于开源7B规模基线模型取得了一致改进，其中情感识别提升4.66%，时序敏感任务提升14.29%。

Conclusion: AffectOmni通过显式强化人物中心证据选择和时序推理结构，结合更优的评分机制，有效提升了MLLM在情感推理任务上的准确性和可验证性，证明了GRPO框架与专用奖励设计在情感推理领域的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance on VQA and scene understanding, yet affective reasoning remains vulnerable to shortcut behavior. Models may predict correct answers while neglecting people-centric cues such as micro expressions and body language, which weakens traceability and external verification. Prior reinforcement learning approaches mainly reward context or logical coherence without explicitly enforcing attention to human evidence. In addition, LLM as a Judge scoring often suffers from score clustering, which reduces reward discriminability. We propose AffectOmni, a GRPO trained framework for verifiable affective reasoning. AffectOmni introduces People Focus and Temporal Order rewards to encourage people-centric evidence selection and temporally structured reasoning, and it adopts within-group comparative scoring to produce more stable and discriminative reward signals. For verification, a Thinking Summarizer converts free form rationales into executable evidence instructions, which are grounded into pixel level evidence regions via SAM3 to provide an externally auditable interface outside the training loop. Experiments on IntentBench, Daily Omni, and WorldSense show consistent improvements over open source 7B scale baselines, including gains of 4.66% on emotion recognition and +14.29% on temporally sensitive tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/eliot127825-rgb/AffectOmni_nobody.

</details>


### [253] [Agentic AI for operating scientific instruments for nanoscale characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26198)
*Zahra Ayar,Marcos Penedo,Mahdi Mehdikhani,Nahid Hosseini,Prabhu Prasad Swain,Georg E. Fantner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于代理式AI的原子力显微镜（AFM）操作系统框架，通过三个MCP代理（AFM Messenger、AFM Pilot、AFM Doctor）连接大语言模型与仪器功能，实现自然语言指令到仪器命令的转换、图像质量评估与参数自适应调整、图像伪影诊断与后处理，并通过歧义检查层确保硬件操作安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学仪器（如AFM）操作需要持续的专业决策，现有自动化方案通常只能通过硬编码例程或专用模型覆盖工作流的部分环节，缺乏一个通用的、无需针对特定样本或成像模式重新训练的智能代理系统。

Method: 提出基于MCP的三代理框架：AFM Messenger将自然语言指令转换为经过校验的仪器命令；AFM Pilot通过LLM评估图像质量并调整成像参数；AFM Doctor诊断图像伪影并执行预审批工具集中的后处理。在硬件执行前加入歧义检查层保障安全操作。

Result: 与微调和现成工具使用模型的基准测试表明，守护执行层而非模型能力本身将错误命令执行降至零。在多种样本的实机实验中，AFM Pilot在图像质量、迭代次数和调参时间上与专家操作员无显著差异。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了科学仪器代理化操作的安全路径，实验意图仍由人类定义，而命令执行、基于图像的调参和后处理可委托给AI代理。

Abstract: Operating a scientific instrument such as an atomic force microscope (AFM) requires continuous expert decision-making. A trained user defines the experimental intent, translates it into instrument commands, assesses incoming data, adjusts imaging parameters, and post-processes the final image. Existing automation usually addresses only parts of this workflow through hard-coded routines, task-specific controllers, or trained machine-learning models. Here we present an agentic-AI framework that operates the executable part of the AFM workflow using a general-purpose, tool-augmented large language model connected to instrument functions through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). The framework consists of 3 MCP-based agents: AFM Messenger converts natural-language instructions into checked instrument commands; AFM Pilot assesses image quality through a large language model (LLM) and, if necessary, adapts imaging parameters; and AFM Doctor diagnoses image artifacts and applies transparent post-processing from a pre-approved tool set. Because the language model performs image assessment rather than a fixed scalar objective or external optimizer, the same strategy can be applied across sample types and imaging modes without specific retraining. Safe hardware operation is enforced through an ambiguity check layer before execution. Benchmarking against fine-tuned and off-the-shelf tool-using models shows that this guarded execution layer, rather than model capability alone, reduces wrong-command execution to zero. In live experiments on different samples, AFM Pilot matched expert operators in image quality, iteration count, and tuning time, with no significant difference. These results demonstrate a safe route to agentic operation of scientific instruments, where experimental intent remains human-defined while command execution, image-based tuning, and post-processing are delegated to AI agents.

</details>


### [254] [Benchmarking AI Agents for Hardware Design Automation via MCP Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26199)
*Leonardo Liparulo,Francesco Pierri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了本地部署的大语言模型智能体在行业真实硬件设计工具调用场景中的可靠性，构建了基于MCP协议的基准测试，评估了七个开源模型在不同配置下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件设计工作流中工程师频繁执行依赖顺序的操作（如建组件、加端口、接线），但专有组件规格和命名约定的保密性限制了使用托管专有API，需要本地部署模型，但其在工具调用环境中的可靠性尚不清楚。

Method: 构建了一个复现专有硬件设计工具状态和依赖逻辑的MCP服务器，设计了涵盖单操作编辑、多步依赖链、无效请求、拼写错误提示和多服务器工具上下文的基准测试，评估七个开源模型在不同系统提示、工具描述详细程度、上下文范围和单/多智能体架构下的表现。

Result: 强模型在基准工作流上可达到接近完全的预期调用覆盖率，但可靠性强烈依赖任务结构和智能体配置。详尽的工具描述持续减少失败；少样本提示对某些模型导致严重不行动；累积上下文损害受限模型；多智能体分解帮助弱模型或长会话，但增加额外调用成本。

Conclusion: 研究为在状态化硬件设计环境中部署本地LLM智能体提供了实用指导，揭示了模型选择与配置之间的关键权衡。

Abstract: We ask whether AI agents powered by locally deployed large language models can reliably automate expert-defined hardware design workflows in an industry-realistic tool-calling setting. In these environments, engineers issue repetitive, dependency-ordered operations---such as creating components, adding ports, and wiring connections---through specialised tools. Confidentiality constraints on component specifications and naming conventions often preclude hosted proprietary APIs, motivating the use of locally deployed models. To study this setting, we build a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server that reproduces the state and dependency logic of a proprietary hardware design tool used in embedded system development and construct a benchmark covering single-operation edits, multi-step dependency chains, invalid requests, misspelled prompts, and multi-server tool contexts. We evaluate seven open-source models comparing pipeline choices including system prompts, tool-description detail, context scope, and single-agent versus multi-agent architectures. Results show that strong models can achieve near-complete expected-call coverage on the benchmarked workflows, but reliability depends strongly on both task structure and agent configuration. Comprehensive tool descriptions consistently reduce failures, few-shot prompting can cause severe inaction for some models, cumulative context harms constrained models, and multi-agent decomposition helps weak workers or long sessions at the cost of additional calls. These findings provide practical guidance for deploying local LLM agents in stateful hardware design environments.

</details>


### [255] [GameWAM: A World Action Model for Video Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26200)
*Yuncheng Guo,Zhanqiu Zhang,Yiwen Guo,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GameWAM是首个用于原生闭环游戏玩法与GUI控制的World-Action模型（WAM），通过并行视觉与动作生成过程联合预测未来视觉观测和可执行的键盘鼠标轨迹，在长时序交互中展现出竞争力的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏智能体直接映射视觉与任务上下文到动作但缺乏显式世界动态建模，而交互式游戏世界模型虽能预测视觉未来却无法作为任务策略使用。WAMs虽将两者统一，但在视频游戏的动态与开放交互场景下尚未被充分探索。

Method: GameWAM通过块因果条件化的并行视觉与动作生成过程联合生成未来视觉观测与可执行键盘鼠标轨迹；构建同步的游戏玩法与GUI轨迹用于联合学习；在每个动作步预测游戏/GUI模式以处理异构原生控制；采用块循环控制实现长时序交互，执行短动作前缀后基于新观测重规划。

Result: 实验表明GameWAM在任务成功率上具有竞争力，且执行的原生动作数少于对比智能体。此外发现了低频动作源印记（LASI）现象，即采样动作源的低频分量会系统性地影响生成相机运动，揭示生成式控制中的源敏感失效模式。

Conclusion: GameWAM证明了World-Action模型在视频游戏原生闭环控制中的可行性，但同时也揭示了生成式控制中动作源敏感性的新问题，为后续研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Modern video games combine first-person perception, rapid visual changes, persistent world state, and heterogeneous native controls. Existing game agents map visual and task context directly to actions but lack explicit world dynamics modeling, whereas interactive game world models predict visual futures from supplied actions but do not serve as task policies. World-Action Models (WAMs) unify these objectives, but remain largely unexplored under the dynamics and open-ended interaction of video games. We introduce GameWAM, to our knowledge the first WAM for native closed-loop gameplay and GUI control. GameWAM jointly generates future visual observations and executable keyboard-mouse trajectories through parallel visual and action generative processes with block-causal conditioning and flow matching. To support joint world-action learning, we construct synchronized gameplay and GUI trajectories. To handle heterogeneous native control, GameWAM predicts a gameplay/GUI mode at each action step and generates actions with mode-specific prediction distributions and continuous-action normalization. For long-horizon interaction, block-cycle control predicts beyond the committed horizon, executes only a short action prefix, and replans from new observations, while fine-grained within-cycle context and hierarchical cross-cycle history preserve temporal continuity. Experiments demonstrate competitive task success with fewer executed native actions than the compared agents. We further uncover Low-Frequency Action Source Imprinting (LASI), in which low-frequency components of the sampled action source systematically steer coarse generated camera motion under fixed conditioning, revealing a source-sensitivity failure mode in generative control. Project page is available at https://yunncheng.github.io/GameWAM/.

</details>


### [256] [Same Model, Different Harness: Different Coding-Agent Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26218)
*Sydney Lewis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究编码智能体中"模型"与"脚手架（harness）"的耦合关系，发现当固定模型和任务、仅改变脚手架配置时，模型的表现结果会发生显著变化，因此编码智能体评估应将模型与脚手架视为一个整体求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前编码智能体评估往往只关注模型本身的性能，但实际系统中模型与harness（决定模型看到什么、使用什么工具、如何继续工作）是紧密耦合的。作者希望探究：在模型和任务固定不变的情况下，改变harness是否会影响最终结果。

Method: 在同一harness上设置两种配置进行对比实验：控制组按时间顺序提供完整对话，处理组在上下文填满时机械缩短较早的工具结果，并响应重复或停滞的工作。在三个编码基准（SWE-bench Verified、SWE-bench Pro、FeatureBench）上比较两种配置的效果，并在不同上下文窗口（紧窗口20,480 token和宽窗口）下进行测试。

Result: 在紧上下文条件下，处理配置在三个压力比较中均提高了平均每任务fail-to-pass比例（F2PF），并在SWE-bench Verified和SWE-bench Pro上增加了完整解决方案。紧窗口对比（169个任务、20,480 token窗口、固定480秒）中，平均F2PF从28%提升至49%，完整解决方案从43提升至72。同一冻结处理配置在另外三个不同模型上也提升了性能。宽窗口Qwen3.6比较中，Verified和Pro上的观测结果接近，而FeatureBench在处理配置下保持更高的平均F2PF，且处理配置的每轮prompt token更少。

Conclusion: 改变harness可以改变未改变权重的模型所能达到的结果，说明编码智能体的评估应将模型与harness作为一个整体来测试和报告，而非仅关注模型本身。

Abstract: A coding agent combines a model with a harness, which decides what the model sees, which tools it can use, and how the work continues. We ask whether changing the harness changes the result when the model and task stay fixed. We compare two configurations of the same harness on three coding benchmarks. The control supplies the full conversation in time order, while the treatment keeps the same record but mechanically shortens older tool results as the context fills and responds to repeated or stalled work.
  Under tight context, the treatment raises mean per-task fail-to-pass fraction (F2PF) in all three pressure comparisons and increases complete solutions on SWE-bench Verified and SWE-bench Pro. The tight-window Verified comparison uses 169 tasks, a 20,480-token window, and a fixed 480-second attempt endpoint; on this cohort, treatment raises mean per-task F2PF from 28 percent to 49 percent and complete solutions from 43 to 72. Without model-specific retuning, the same frozen treatment also raises both endpoints on the same cohort for three additional models with different designs. In the wide-window Qwen3.6 comparisons, observed arm outcomes are close on Verified and Pro, while FeatureBench retains a higher mean per-task F2PF under treatment. On the wide-window Verified cohort, treatment also serves fewer prompt tokens per turn. Because changing the harness changed what unchanged model weights could accomplish, coding-agent evaluations should treat the model and harness together as the tested solver.

</details>


### [257] [LLM Agents for Time-Series: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26226)
*Yilong Chen,Xiao Qin,Chenghao Liu,Liang Wu,Noelle I. Samia,Kaize Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于LLM智能体在时间序列问题中应用的综述，采用问题驱动的分类法，将现有系统分为预测与推理、增强与合成、异常检测与诊断、决策支持四类，并比较了各系统中的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在时间序列问题中的应用日益增多，但不同任务场景下的设计选择差异很大，缺乏一个系统性的、以问题为导向的梳理框架来指导研究和实践。

Method: 采用问题驱动的分类法，按时间序列问题类型而非孤立的技术组件来组织系统分类；在每类中分析任务需求如何影响智能体架构、工具使用和记忆设计；汇总代表性数据集与环境，并在共享或相近设置下比较模型性能。

Result: 提出了四类LLM智能体时间序列系统（预测与推理、增强与合成、异常检测与诊断、决策支持），汇总了代表性数据集和环境，并对比了相近设置下的模型性能。

Conclusion: 该综述为设计面向时间序列问题的LLM智能体提供了以任务为导向的指导，并指出了未来研究中的开放缺口。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly being developed for time-series problems, but their design choices vary substantially across task settings. This survey adopts a problem-driven taxonomy that organizes these systems by the time-series problems they address rather than by isolated technical components. We group existing systems into four categories: forecasting and reasoning, augmentation and synthesis, anomaly detection and diagnosis, and decision support. Within each category, we examine how task requirements shape agent architecture, tool use, and memory design. We further summarize representative datasets and environments, and compare reported model performance under shared or closely related settings. Overall, this survey offers a task-oriented guide to designing LLM-based agents for time-series problems and identifies open gaps for future work.

</details>


### [258] [The Reasoning Tax: Token Economics of LLM Reasoning Across Task Types and Deployment Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26235)
*Sachin Gopal Wani,Ajay Dholakia,David Ellison*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Token Economy Score (TES)这一边际基准指标，用于衡量推理模型相对于非推理基线的准确率增益与生成token倍数之间的关系，并基于151个模型-基准评估运行的数据分析，发现任务结构比名义难度更能预测推理效率，且推理存在边际收益递减现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅以准确率基准评估推理能力的大语言模型，忽略了扩展思考token何时值得其成本这一关键部署问题。

Method: 提出Token Economy Score (TES)边际基准指标，定义paired和approximated两种变体，跨7个基准（数学、代码生成、科学推理、指令遵循、专家知识等）进行151次模型-基准评估运行的实证分析，并引入Reasoning Cost Share (RCS)和Deployment Cost Multiplier (DCM)分析部署经济学。

Result: 任务结构能预测推理效率：顺序推理链任务（如AIME 2025和LiveCodeBench）TES高，而知识回忆任务（如MMLU-Pro）即使困难TES也低；更高推理努力水平存在系统性边际收益递减，甚至出现额外思考降低准确率的情况；推理成本往往由内部思考主导。

Conclusion: 推理模式应基于任务类型、努力水平和部署上下文有选择地启用，而非视为普遍有益的模式。

Abstract: Accuracy-only benchmarking of reasoning-capable large language models misses a central deployment question: when do extended thinking tokens earn their cost? We introduce the Token Economy Score (TES), a marginal benchmarking metric that measures the accuracy gain of a reasoning model over a non-reasoning baseline, normalized by the generated-token multiplier. We define paired and approximated TES variants for model families with reasoning toggles and frontier models without direct non-reasoning counterparts. We then conduct an empirical benchmarking analysis across 151 model-benchmark evaluation runs on seven benchmarks spanning mathematics, code generation, science reasoning, instruction following, expert knowledge, knowledge recall, and research-level physics. The analysis examines three deployment-facing dimensions: which task structures yield positive marginal reasoning efficiency, how increasing reasoning effort changes TES within model families, and how deployment context changes economic viability. Results show that task structure predicts reasoning efficiency better than nominal difficulty: sequential inferencechain tasks such as AIME 2025 and LiveCodeBench show high TES, while knowledge-recall tasks such as MMLU-Pro show low TES despite their difficulty. We also find systematic diminishing returns at higher reasoning effort levels, including cases where additional thinking reduces accuracy. Finally, Reasoning Cost Share (RCS) shows that inference spend is often dominated by internal thinking, while Deployment Cost Multiplier (DCM) shows how on-premises deployment can change the economics of otherwise costly reasoning workloads. These findings support a benchmarking-driven model-selection rule: enable reasoning selectively by task type, effort level, and deployment context rather than treating it as a universally beneficial mode.

</details>


### [259] [6.5% of the Neuro-Symbolic Literature Can Be Reproduced from Its Published Artifacts, a Six-Stage Audit Framework and First Instantiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26236)
*Brandon Colelough,Vladimir Martirosyan,Ishan Tamrakar,William Regli,Aditya Kumar,Anh N. Nhu,Dhruv Dubey,Raj Ambavane,Haowei Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个六阶段的科学声明可复现性审计框架，并在神经符号AI（NSAI）子领域进行了实例化。审计发现1,304篇合格论文中仅455篇有可验证的公开代码工件，最终成功复现85项研究（占合格语料6.52%），揭示了严重的可复现性赤字。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学领域存在严重的可复现性问题，即使论文声明"代码可得"也常常无法复现。作者希望通过系统性审计框架量化这一问题，并推动学术出版规范改进。

Method: 提出了六阶段审计框架：(1) 检索文献并去重；(2) 标题和摘要筛选；(3) 全文本筛查；(4) 寻找可验证的公开代码工件；(5-6) 对符合条件的论文进行部分或完全复现。在NSAI子领域实例化该框架进行多年期审计。

Result: 检索5,497条记录，经过去重和筛选后1,304篇合格。其中849篇无公开代码工件，仅455篇进入工件清单。最终完全或部分复现85项研究（合格语料的6.52%，尝试复现的18.68%）。321次复现尝试因缺失非代码工件受阻，42次因缺失或不可用的代码仓库受阻。

Conclusion: NSAI领域存在持续且严重的可复现性赤字，即使"代码可用"声明也未能保证可复现性。作者主张实证NSAI论文应在投稿时提供完整、带版本且永久存档的工件包。

Abstract: We present a six-stage framework for auditing the reproducibility of scientific claims across a research literature within the computer science domain, and instantiate our framework for the neuro-symbolic AI (NSAI) subdomain. Instantiating the framework on the NSAI subdomain produced a multi-year audit. Stage one retrieved 5,497 records and removed 3,018 duplicates. Stage two screened the 2,479 unique records at title and abstract, identifying 1,365 self-identified NSAI records, then removed a further 61 at full text for off-topic, non-research, no-quantitative-evaluation, or inaccessible-full-text reasons. Stage three sought a verifiable public code artifact for each of the 1,304 eligible records and found none for 849, leaving 455 to enter the artifact inventory and bounded rerun of stages four and five. We fully or partially reproduced 85 studies, 6.52% of the eligible corpus and 18.68% of attempted reruns. We found that 321 attempted reruns were blocked by missing non- code artifacts and 42 by missing or unusable code repositories. These figures quantify a persistent reproducibility deficit that survives even nominal "code available" declarations, and signal the need for enforced, versioned, and permanently archived artifact bundles in future NSAI publications. We argue that empirical NSAI papers should be required at submission time to provide complete, versioned, and permanently archived artifact bundles.

</details>


### [260] [Assessing mentalization in humans and large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26291)
*Aamir Sohail,Xintong Zhong,Arkady Konovalov,Patricia L. Lockwood,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过两种经济博弈游戏和认知计算建模，系统评估了多个LLM家族（DeepSeek、GPT-4.1、GPT-5、Gemini 2.0 Flash）的心智化能力，并与人类参与者进行对比，发现不同模型的推理深度存在显著差异，GPT-5甚至超越人类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有研究表明LLM在心理理论任务上表现出与人类一致的行为，但尚无研究探讨这些模型能否通过心智化来引导自适应行为，即利用对他人的信念和意图的推断来优化自身决策。

Method: 使用两种经济博弈游戏（N=2,099个LLM智能体）配合认知计算建模，涵盖DeepSeek、GPT-4.1、GPT-5和Gemini 2.0 Flash四个模型家族，针对不同复杂度的对手进行测试，并对比人类参与者（N=251）的表现，同时检验策略提示对推理的改善效果。

Result: LLM在两种博弈中均表现出清晰的心智化行为与计算特征，但不同模型提供商和模型大小之间差异显著；策略提示总体上有助于提升推理质量，但收益在不同任务间存在差异；GPT-5能灵活调整递归推理深度以适应越来越复杂的对手，表现优于人类参与者。

Conclusion: 不同LLM在心智化能力上存在显著差异，认知计算建模可作为评估人类与机器比较智能的正式方法。

Abstract: Mentalization - the ability to infer others' beliefs and intentions to guide one's own choices - is a key cognitive function underlying human social interactions. Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate behaviour consistent with humans on theory-of-mind tasks, yet whether these models can guide adaptive behaviour through mentalization is unknown. Here we use two economic games with cognitive computational modeling to uncover the latent strategies underlying mentalization in LLMs. We tested individual LLM agents across four model families, DeepSeek, GPT-4.1, GPT-5 and Gemini 2.0 Flash (N = 2,099), against opponents of varying sophistication and examined whether a prompting strategy designed to elicit strategic reasoning improved performance. We benchmarked results against human participants (N = 251) as a comparative measure. Across both games, LLMs showed clear behavioural and computational signatures of mentalizing that differed markedly by model provider and size. Strategic prompting generally improved performance by inducing more sophisticated reasoning, yet the extent of the benefit differed across the two tasks. Last, GPT-5 agents flexibly adapted their recursive depth of reasoning to increasingly sophisticated opponents, demonstrating superior performance to human participants. Collectively, we demonstrate different capacities for mentalization across LLMs, and highlight cognitive computational modeling as a formal method for assessing comparative intelligence across humans and machines.

</details>


### [261] [Approved Too Late: Verdict Staleness in LLM-Guarded Self-Adaptive Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26306)
*Ilai Shraga,Roei Eshel,Lior Gorelik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM护栏在自适应系统中的"新鲜度"问题，即审批在检查时正确但执行时已过时的TOCTOU风险。提出Freshness-Bounded Shield (FBS)方法，在不依赖显式动力学模型的情况下估计审批的有效性时间范围，将审批过期率从3.4-24.7%降至0-1.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM防护栏为自适应系统（SAS）提供审批时，审批在检查时刻是正确的，但到实际执行时可能已经过时或失效，形成Execute阶段的TOCTOU（检查时间到使用时间）安全隐患。现有研究忽视了这种"审批新鲜度"问题。

Method: 论文区分三种度量审批新鲜度的量：固定动作重放下的全候选审批变化率、记录闭环轨迹上oracle标注的审批过期率、以及judge条件的使用时无效性。在此基础上提出Freshness-Bounded Shield (FBS)，利用安全侧边际和近期特征波动性来估计每个审批的有效性时间范围，无需显式植物动力学模型。同时审计了四个LLM judge的使用时无效性。

Result: 在五个可复现的SAS环境中，在常见8步重放偏移下，全候选审批变化率范围为5.3-48.4%。FBS在固定设置下将oracle标注的审批过期率从3.4-24.7%降至0-1.8%。审计发现四个LLM judge在每个审批流中都存在非零的judge条件使用时无效性。

Conclusion: 论文提出"新鲜度契约"概念：每个审批必须在检查时正确且在使时仍然有效。FBS是缓解LLM护栏TOCTOU风险的有效方法，为自适应系统中LLM安全防护提供了新的保障维度。

Abstract: A large language model (LLM) guardrail for a self-adaptive system (SAS) may issue an approval that is correct at check time but stale by actuation. This creates an Execute-stage time-of-check to time-of-use (TOCTOU) hazard. We study verdict freshness: whether a guardrail verdict remains valid when used. We distinguish three quantities that answer different questions: all-candidate verdict change under fixed-action replay, oracle-labeled approval expiry on recorded closed-loop trajectories, and judge-conditioned use-time invalidity. Across five reproducible SAS environments, all-candidate verdict-change rates span 5.3-48.4% at a common replay shift of eight simulator steps. We introduce the Freshness-Bounded Shield (FBS), which estimates each approval's validity horizon from its safe-side margin and recent feature volatility, without an explicit plant-dynamics model. Using fixed settings documented in the artifact, FBS reduces oracle-labeled approval-expiry rates from 3.4-24.7% to 0-1.8% at the same shift. A separate audit of four LLM judges finds nonzero judge-conditioned use-time invalidity in every approval stream. We formulate a freshness contract: every approval must be correct at check time and remain valid at use time.

</details>


### [262] [FaithSieve: Fine-Grained Evaluation of Math Proofs with Faithful Formal Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26310)
*Ziyu Wang,Qiming Dai,Yishan Wu,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍FaithSieve，一个基于Lean的框架，用于细粒度评估自然语言数学证明，通过分解证明步骤、提取类型化证明义务并进行形式化验证，显著提升首次错误定位的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学证明评估方法依赖基于模型的自然语言判断，容易忽视局部推理漏洞；形式化定理证明器虽可严格验证，但存在局部性和语义不匹配问题，即证明器可能通过证明过宽目标绕过局部缺陷，或验证偏离原始数学意图的自动形式化陈述。

Method: FaithSieve框架将粗粒度证明步骤分解为局部推理单元，提取类型化证明义务，并通过形式化评估代理进行验证。形式化验证由语义对齐评分门控，仅当形式化陈述忠实保留原文的上下文、对象和逻辑形式时才纳入Lean证据。

Result: 在350道题的奥林匹克数据集上，FaithSieve使用GPT-5.4骨干达到81.43%的精确首次错误准确率，优于直接判定基线的72.29%。在200道题的大学数据集（涵盖六个高级领域）上达到84.5%的精确准确率，对比直接判定器的75.0%。

Conclusion: 将证明分解为细粒度单元并基于忠实的正式证据进行验证，能够显著提升自然语言推理的可靠评估。

Abstract: Large language models can now generate complex, multi-step mathematical proofs, but reliably determining their correctness and localizing early logical errors remains a critical challenge. Existing evaluation approaches largely depend on model-based natural-language judgments, which often overlook local reasoning gaps. While formal theorem provers like Lean offer a path to rigorous verification, using them to evaluate informal text requires solving locality and semantic mismatches: a prover might bypass a local flaw by proving an overly broad target, or validate an auto-formalized statement that drifts from the original mathematical intent. To address this, we introduce FaithSieve, a Lean-assisted framework for fine-grained evaluation of natural-language mathematical proofs. FaithSieve decomposes coarse proof steps into local reasoning units, extracts typed proof obligations, and verifies them through a formal evaluation agent. Formal validation is gated by semantic alignment scoring, so Lean evidence is incorporated only when the formal statement faithfully preserves the context, objects, and logical form of the original claim. We construct two expert-verified datasets, ProofLoc-Olympiad and ProofLoc-University, to benchmark first-error localization. On the 350-problem Olympiad dataset, FaithSieve using a GPT-5.4 backbone achieves 81.43% exact first-error accuracy, outperforming the direct-judging baseline of 72.29%. Furthermore, on the 200-problem ProofLoc-University benchmark spanning six advanced domains, FaithSieve reaches 84.5% exact accuracy, compared to 75.0% for the direct judge. Our work demonstrates that decomposing proofs into fine-grained units and grounding them with faithful formal evidence significantly improves reliable evaluation of natural-language reasoning.

</details>


### [263] [ProofEvolve: Neuro-Symbolic Evolution for Formal Automated Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26334)
*Wenqian Ye,Ziwei Guan,Eric Xie,Bohan Liu,Shivani Modi,Buyun Zhang,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Henry Kautz,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProofEvolve是一个神经符号框架，通过演化显式的、经过形式验证的符号证明结构（AND-OR证明DAG），结合神经模型提出变异算子（分解、修复、模式重组），在Lean内核验证下实现自动化定理证明的递归自我改进，并在三个竞赛级Lean基准上取得最高平均求解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经证明器未能充分保留递归自我改进结构：要么通过昂贵的权重更新将证明经验嵌入模型参数，要么仅将验证过的中间推导保留在当前问题内；且方法严重依赖稀疏的全证明反馈，忽略了失败的局部尝试中的有用发现。

Method: 提出ProofEvolve框架：(1)神经模型提出变异算子（分解、修复、模式重组）；(2)符号Lean内核验证每个证明步骤；(3)在问题内，演化带行为索引档案的部分AND-OR证明DAG；(4)跨问题，通过内核检查的模式提取将新证明的子DAG加入持久模式库；(5)通过类型化的模式重组继承已解结果，每个剩余前提暴露为新子目标。

Result: 在三个竞赛级Lean基准上，ProofEvolve在评估的证明系统中取得了最高平均求解率。

Conclusion: ProofEvolve成功地将递归自我改进融入自动化定理证明：从失败尝试中保留已验证结果并用于后续证明，同时不削弱形式可靠性，实现了知识边界的有效扩展。

Abstract: Automated theorem proving offers a natural foundation for recursive self-improvement in scientific discovery. However, existing neural provers do not fully preserve this recursive structure, where the learning process should be self-improving over time. Existing methods either embed proof experience into model parameters through expensive weight updates, or keep verified intermediate deductions only within the current problem. In addition, these methods also heavily rely on sparse whole-proof feedback, even when unsuccessful partial attempts contain useful discoveries. To close the gap, we propose ProofEvolve, a neuro-symbolic framework that evolves explicit, formally verified symbolic proof structures with neural models to decisively expand the knowledge boundary. In this framework, the neural model proposes variation operators, including decompositions, repairs, and schema recombinations. The symbolic Lean kernel verifies every proof transition. Over the evolution loops, ProofEvolve computes verified closure over the resulting proof directed acyclic graphs (DAGs). Within each problem, ProofEvolve evolves partial AND-OR proof DAGs in a behaviorally indexed archive. Across problems, kernel-checked schema extraction adds newly proved sub-DAGs to a persistent schema library. Proof DAGs inherit the solved results through typed schema recombination, with every residual premise exposed as a new subgoal. This evolutionary process preserves verified results from incomplete attempts and makes them available for later proofs without weakening formal soundness. Across three competition-level Lean benchmarks, ProofEvolve achieves the highest average solve rate among the evaluated proof systems.

</details>


### [264] [Fine-Tuning of Transformer models with Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26430)
*Harshavardhan Adepu,Li Zhang,Sanjiv Kumar,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FrameFT，一种基于Fusion Frame基的稀疏系数矩阵参数高效微调方法，通过算法生成可跨层共享的基，仅需存储稀疏系数即可大幅降低内存和计算开销，在语言和视觉任务上达到或超越现有PEFT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法（如LoRA）的内存开销随模型规模线性增长（O(dr)），在大规模预训练模型微调场景下仍存在较高的内存和计算成本。

Method: FrameFT将参数更新ΔW建模为Fusion Frame基上的稀疏系数矩阵展开。Fusion Frame可算法生成并在模型层间共享，仅优化和存储稀疏系数，从而显著减少内存占用。稀疏系数矩阵结构和Fusion Frame的稀疏性带来计算效率提升，并提供形式化收敛性分析。

Result: 在监督微调基准（包括语言和视觉任务）上，FrameFT以远少于现有PEFT方法的可训练参数数量，达到或超越了当前最优的PEFT性能。

Conclusion: FrameFT通过Fusion Frame稀疏表示有效解决了PEFT的内存瓶颈，在保持性能的同时大幅降低参数量和计算成本，是一种高效的模型微调方案。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) strategies such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) are effective solutions for fine-tuning large-scale pre-trained models; however, their memory requirements scale with the size of the model, $\mathcal{O}(dr)$, where $d$ is the model's hidden dimension and $r$ is the rank. Our proposal, FrameFT, models the parameter update $ΔW$ with a sparse coefficient matrix in a Fusion Frame basis. Fusion Frames can be generated algorithmically and shared across model layers, enabling very efficient updates. Only the sparse coefficients of the basis expansion are stored/optimized, reducing the memory footprint. The sparse structure of the coefficient matrix in FrameFT and the sparsity in the Fusion Frames give large compute benefits, and our analysis provides formal convergence results. We evaluate the idea across a suite of supervised fine-tuning benchmarks, focusing on language tasks, but also report application to vision models. Our experiments show that FrameFT achieves performance on par with/exceeding state-of-the-art PEFT techniques, but needs far fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [265] [Don't Overthink, Don't Underthink: Toward Adaptive Reasoning in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26442)
*Md Jueal Mia,M. Hadi Amini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究智能体AI系统中推理资源分配不当的问题，区分过度推理和不足推理两种失败模式，并通过MATH-500和GAIA基准实验证明过度推理导致计算浪费而不足推理导致解决方案不完整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理方法多依赖固定或预分配的推理控制策略，但真实智能体工作流中推理需求是动态变化的，固定策略会导致推理过度或不足，构成下一代智能体AI的核心挑战。

Method: 在MATH-500和GAIA基准上评估推理分配问题，使用工具决策延迟、token消耗、token限额耗尽和答案正确性等指标，对过度推理和不足推理案例进行分类量化分析。

Result: 过度推理案例伴随更高的计算成本但无成比例的准确率提升；不足推理案例则一贯与错误或不完整的解决方案相关。

Conclusion: 智能体AI的关键挑战不是模型应进行多少推理，而是如何根据动态任务需求自适应分配推理资源，这为未来自适应推理机制研究提供了动机。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown that increased inference-time reasoning can improve performance on complex tasks. However, many existing approaches rely on fixed or preallocated reasoning controls, such as fixed token budgets, pre-execution difficulty estimates, or activation-space interventions, and are often evaluated on standalone reasoning benchmarks rather than full agentic workflows. These assumptions may not hold in agentic AI systems, where reasoning requirements evolve dynamically through planning, tool use, memory retrieval, and agent-to-agent interactions. Consequently, reasoning can become either excessive or insufficient, resulting in unnecessary computation, increased latency, planning drift, excessive tool use, or incomplete solutions. We argue that a major challenge for next-generation agentic AI is not merely how much reasoning a language model should perform, but how it should allocate reasoning according to evolving task demands. We characterize over-reasoning and under-reasoning as recurring failure modes of misallocated reasoning and evaluate them on MATH-500 and the GAIA public validation benchmark. Using tool-decision latency, token consumption, token-limit exhaustion, and answer correctness, our results suggest that cases classified as over-reasoning are associated with higher computational cost without proportional accuracy gains, whereas cases classified as under-reasoning are consistently associated with incorrect or incomplete solutions. These findings motivate future research on adaptive reasoning mechanisms for agentic AI.

</details>


### [266] [PILOT in the Loop: Live Self-Improvement for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26530)
*Yang Xiao,Yusong Sun,Haoyi Wu,Wenyang Hui,Wen Da,Zhaokai Luo,Mu Chuan,Yao Hu,Wenjie Li,Chengyue Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PILOT，一个监督者-工作者架构的智能体框架，通过实时引导（live steering）和实时自我进化（live self-evolution）两种机制实现持续运行的自我改进，在多个基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进方法仅在执行结束后处理经验，无法实时调整当前运行或立即验证从运行中学到的教训。作者认为自我改进应该是实时的（live），既需要引导当前活动运行，也需要更新持久化工具链。

Method: 提出PILOT，采用监督者-工作者（supervisor-worker）架构，包含两个耦合机制：(1) 实时引导——独立的监督者可在执行过程中重定向或中止活动的工作者；(2) 实时自我进化——将执行过程中揭示的程序和失败模式蒸馏为可复用的技能和记忆。

Result: 在两个固定主干和三个基准上，PILOT在六个配置中有五个排名第一。在Terminal-Bench 2.0上，PILOT比对照组最高提升9.8个百分点。自我改进设置下，GLM-5.1提升14.6分，Kimi-K2.6提升12.4分。平均输出token减少42.9%和47.4%，每百万输出token的成功评估分别提升110.3%和134.0%。

Conclusion: PILOT证明了实时自我改进的有效性，通过监督者-工作者分离架构同时实现活动运行的重定向和经验的即时沉淀，在性能和效率两方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Long-horizon agent runs generate experience that can improve both the current run and future work. Most self-improvement methods process this experience only after execution ends, so they cannot redirect the active run or immediately apply and validate lessons learned from it. We argue that self-improvement should instead be live, using emerging experience both to redirect the active run and to update the persistent harness. Existing agent architectures do not fully support this goal. Single-agent self-correction combines task execution and trajectory assessment within one context, while subagent delegation separates execution but typically cannot redirect an active subagent. We present PILOT, a supervisor-worker harness for live self-improvement through two coupled mechanisms: (1) live steering lets a separate supervisor redirect or abort the active worker during execution; and (2) live self-evolution distils procedures and failure modes revealed during execution into reusable skills and memory. Across two frozen backbones and three benchmarks, PILOT ranks first in five of six configurations. On Terminal-Bench 2.0, PILOT outperforms counterpart harnesses by up to 9.8 percentage points. In the self-improvement setting, PILOT gains 14.6 points with GLM-5.1 and 12.4 points with Kimi-K2.6. Mean output tokens fall by 42.9% and 47.4%, while successful evaluations per million output tokens rise by 110.3% and 134.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [267] [Multi2AV-Safety: Benchmarking Safety in Multimodal-to-Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26535)
*Kaichao Jiang,Changtao Miao,Baiqi Wu,Zhiyuan Lu,Kang Yang,Peiwei Zhao,Junchi Chen,Yunfeng Diao,He Liu,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Multi2AV-Safety，首个覆盖音频-视频生成中所有11种非单模态条件组合的安全基准，包含11,024个攻击实例，揭示了当前多模态安全防护在组合风险感知方面的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频-视频生成正从提示驱动转向多模态条件驱动，文本、图像、音频和视频可联合塑造生成输出。有害意图可能不再存在于单一输入中，而是从跨模态和跨时间的良性或弱有害条件交互中涌现。现有安全基准主要聚焦于提示层面或固定的条件接口，难以系统研究此类组合风险。

Method: 构建Multi2AV-Safety基准，覆盖T/I/A/V四种模态的所有11种非单模态条件配置，生成11,024个攻击实例，并对代表性多模态安全守卫进行评估测试。

Result: 评估揭示了两种互补的失败模式：良性输入组合可涌现出有害语义；显式有害线索与良性多模态上下文混合时更难被检测。两种模式共同表明安全守卫未能可靠地跨模态和时间整合安全证据。

Conclusion: 组合风险感知是多模态条件音频-视频生成安全防护的核心能力缺口，即使在所有条件输入均可观察的情况下，现有安全守卫仍无法可靠识别组合性风险。

Abstract: Audio-video generation is rapidly moving from prompt-driven synthesis toward multimodal conditioning, where text, images, audio, and video can jointly shape the generated output. This shift changes the nature of safety evaluation: harmful intent may no longer reside in any single input, but instead emerge from how otherwise benign or weakly harmful conditions interact across modalities and time. Existing safety benchmarks, however, remain largely prompt-centric or tied to fixed conditioning interfaces, leaving such compositional risks difficult to study systematically. To bridge this gap, we introduce Multi2AV-Safety, the first safety benchmark, to the best of our knowledge, to cover all 11 non-singleton T/I/A/V conditioning configurations for audio-video generation, comprising 11,024 attack instances. Evaluation on Multi2AV-Safety reveals systematic weaknesses in representative multimodal safety guards across attack mechanisms and harm-evidence structures. Our evaluation reveals two complementary failure modes: harmful semantics can emerge from the combination of individually benign inputs, while explicit harmful cues can become harder to detect when mixed with benign multimodal context. Together, these results identify \emph{compositional risk perception} as a central capability gap in safeguarding multimodal-conditioned audio-video generation: current safety guards fail to reliably integrate safety evidence across modalities and time, even when all conditioning inputs are observable. The dataset will be publicly released in October 2026.

</details>


### [268] [DuMateBench: Evaluating Autonomous Agents in Complex Real-World Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26546)
*Zechun Niu,Yukun Zhao,Jiaxin Zhang,Xu Shen,Jinhua Si,Han Tian,Can Xu,Yunfan Song,Jiaxin Mao,Yansong Gao,Yuchen Li,Jianmin Wu,Lingyong Yan,Shuaiqiang Wang,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DuMateBench，一个基于大规模生产级智能体平台真实用户会话重建的基准测试，包含200个跨8大场景和17个细粒度能力类别的任务，并在隔离Docker容器中注入三种真实环境复杂性（信息不足、不稳定、嘈杂）进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准通常按应用或能力分离任务，且评测环境比实际场景更清洁稳定，无法反映真实世界中多工具工作流的复杂性和环境干扰。

Method: 从大规模生产级智能体平台收集匿名化且经过隐私筛查的用户会话，保留解决方案前的交互历史、持久配置和工作区状态，通过人工验证构建200个任务。在隔离Docker容器中注入三种环境复杂性（Insufficient、Unstable、Noisy），采用确定性评估与LLM-as-Judge混合协议进行评测。

Result: 实验覆盖5个代表性自主智能体框架搭配4个SOTA LLM，揭示了严格任务完成方面的显著差距。鲁棒性、效率和诊断分析表明，环境扰动下的性能由LLM能力和外部智能体框架共同决定。

Conclusion: DuMateBench提供了更贴近真实场景的智能体评测基准，证明了在现实环境复杂性下，LLM与智能体框架的协同对性能至关重要，代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly adopted to complete complex, multi-tool workflows in real-world settings. However, existing benchmarks typically separate tasks by application or capability and evaluate agents in environments that are cleaner and more stable than those encountered in practice. We introduce DuMateBench, a real-session benchmark reconstructed from anonymized and privacy-screened user sessions collected from a large-scale production agent platform. Each task preserves the relevant pre-solution interaction history, persistent configurations, and workspace state, and is then validated through human verification. The resulting benchmark comprises 200 tasks spanning 8 broad scenarios and 17 fine-grained capability categories, with most tasks requiring multiple capability coordination. We execute these tasks in isolated Docker containers injected with three forms of real-world environmental complexity: Insufficient, Unstable, and Noisy, and assess performance using a hybrid deterministic and LLM-as-Judge evaluation protocol. Experiments across five representative autonomous-agent frameworks paired with four state-of-the-art LLMs reveal substantial gaps in strict task completion. Complementary robustness, efficiency, and diagnostic analyses further show that performance under environmental perturbations is jointly shaped by the capabilities of the LLM and the surrounding agent framework. The code and data are publicly available at https://dumatebench.com/.

</details>


### [269] [AgentJudgeBench: A Multi-Difficulty Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Judges on Agentic Tool-Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26623)
*Abhigya Verma,Amit Kumar Saha,Seganrasan Subramanian,Sai Harshitha Aluru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentJudgeBench，首个系统研究LLM裁判在基于工作流DAG的智能体工具调用场景中可靠性的基准测试，包含3,808个实例和多种生成器/裁判组合，发现裁判对齐度随任务难度单调下降，无真实标签时存在77-82%的结构性上限，模型规模无法突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM裁判被广泛用于评估智能体工具调用系统，但其在结构化、依赖驱动的工作流上的可靠性尚未被系统研究。现有LLM-as-a-judge研究多聚焦开放式文本或偏好评估，忽略了智能体工具调用这一重要场景。

Method: 构建AgentJudgeBench基准，包含3,808个实例，覆盖六种DAG拓扑和三个难度等级；使用五个生成器（3B-70B开源模型和GPT-5.4）和六个裁判（20B到前沿规模），在有/无真实标签配对条件下进行系统评估。

Result: 裁判对齐度随任务难度单调下降，无真实标签时退化速度快1.5倍；困难任务无真实标签时所有裁判收敛到77-82%的窄带；真实标签并非一致有益（GPT-5.4降1.5pp，Gemini-2.5-Pro降3.9pp，符合过度锚定效应）；链式推理和温度影响可忽略，结构化评估规则最多提升6.5pp但不普适；有真实标签时QwQ-32B最佳，人类验证显示GPT-OSS-120B最符合人类对齐。

Conclusion: 当前LLM裁判存在根本性局限，模型规模无法克服由任务难度驱动的结构性天花板；研究为智能体系统的可靠评估提供了实用指导。

Abstract: LLM judges are widely used to evaluate agentic tool-calling systems, yet their reliability on structured, dependency-driven workflows remains largely unexamined. We present AgentJudgeBench, the first benchmark to systematically study LLM-as-a-judge reliability for agentic tool-calling over workflow DAGs, as distinct from the broader LLM-as-a-judge task of open-ended text or preference evaluation. The benchmark comprises 3,808 instances spanning six DAG topologies and three difficulty tiers, evaluated with five generators (3B-70B open-weight models and GPT-5.4) and six judges (20B to frontier scale) under paired with- and without-ground-truth conditions. Judge alignment degrades monotonically with task difficulty, 1.5x faster without ground truth, and on hard queries without ground truth all six judges converge to a narrow 77-82% band regardless of scale, revealing a structural ceiling driven primarily by task difficulty, though its height is partly prompt-dependent for weaker generators, that model capacity alone cannot overcome. Ground-truth exposure is not uniformly beneficial: it reduces alignment for GPT-5.4 (1.5 pp) and Gemini-2.5-Pro (3.9 pp), consistent with over-anchoring. Among mitigation strategies, chain-of-thought reasoning and judge temperature both have negligible effect, while structured evaluation rubrics improve alignment by up to 6.5 pp but do not generalize uniformly across judge-generator pairs. With ground truth, QwQ-32B best matches the programmatic reference, while a human validation study identifies GPT-OSS-120B as the most human-aligned judge; without it, frontier judges lead only marginally within the shared ceiling. These results expose fundamental limitations of current LLM judges and yield practical guidelines for reliable evaluation in agentic systems.

</details>


### [270] [Relational Over-Regularization: Graph-Based AI-Generated Text Detection via Sentence Transition Deviation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26694)
*Hyeonchu Park,Bugeun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于句对间关系过度正则化（ROR）信号的AI生成文本检测方法CSFG，通过图神经网络编码跨源风格指纹，在四个基准上达到97.14%的检测准确率，并对未见过的LLM具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGT检测方法依赖token级统计信号或独立的风格特征，容易过拟合特定生成器并在分布偏移下失效，需要更具结构性和鲁棒性的检测信号。

Method: 识别句对级别的结构信号——关系过度正则化（ROR），即LLM生成文本的句间转换方差偏离人类写作，表现为段落边界的重复相似爆发和模板化转换；据此提出Cross-Source Stylometric Fingerprint Graph（CSFG）框架，将位置、顺序、语义和转换偏差信号编码为可学习的GNN边特征，通过每条边的有符号偏差δ_ij操作化ROR。

Result: CSFG在二元检测任务中达到97.14%的准确率，比最强基于图的基线高出11.14个百分点，假阳性率仅1.57%，在通胀方差机制下对未见LLM具有鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: ROR是一种有效的结构级AIGT检测信号，CSFG验证了其可行性；但该方法对转换方差等于或低于人类基线的生成器检测效果会退化，具有一定的适用范围限制。

Abstract: Detecting AI-generated text (AIGT) remains challenging because existing approaches rely on token-level statistical signals or independent stylometric features, causing them to overfit to specific generators and fail under distribution shift. We identify a structural signal at the sentence-pair level: LLMs produce inter-sentence transition variance that deviates from human writing through inflated variance driven by recurring similarity bursts at paragraph boundaries and templated transitions. We formalize this as Relational Over-Regularization (ROR) and validate it across four benchmarks (p < 0.001). The central contribution is this relational problem formulation, not a novel GNN architecture; CSFG is one concrete instantiation for operationalizing ROR. To exploit this signal, we propose the Cross-Source Stylometric Fingerprint Graph (CSFG), a graph-based framework that encodes positional, sequential, semantic, and transition deviation signals as learnable GNN edge features. The per-edge signed deviation δ_ij operationalizes ROR without hand-crafted thresholds and acts as a false-positive calibrator. CSFG achieves 97.14% accuracy under binary detection, outperforming the strongest graph-based baseline by 11.14 pp, with a false-positive rate of 1.57% and robust generalization to unseen LLMs in the inflated-variance regime; detection degrades for generators whose transition variance falls at or below the human baseline.

</details>


### [271] [Five Primitives for Governing Autonomous AI Agents at Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26696)
*Jiten Oswal,John Cadeddu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出企业级自主AI代理部署中，沿用为人类用户设计的控制模型存在三种根本性不匹配，并提出治理自主代理应被视为运行时问题而非模型对齐或构建时问题，据此推导出五个原语（发现、身份、治理、证明、供应链），并描述了相应的实现架构及其代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前企业环境中自主AI代理的治理沿用人类用户和长期服务的控制模型，但代理主体具有短暂性、模型驱动的不可预知行为、以及分布式创建的特点，导致现有控制模型在三个关键方面失效，需要重新思考治理框架。

Method: 作者从"行动生效前和生效后必须回答的问题"出发，推导出五个原语：发现、身份、治理、证明和供应链。每个原语都分析了缺失时的失败后果及为何其他原语无法结构性替代。实现上采用策略中介、按租户动作词表授权、以及哈希链接签名账本（可第三方验证）。

Result: 架构实现上有明确的代价：执行点位于请求关键路径上、身份需要每个工作负载的sidecar、fail-closed中介会将可用性事件转化为拒绝。四个原语已在私有试点中运行，第五个（供应链）作为独立工具构建但尚未集成到请求路径。

Conclusion: 治理自主AI代理是一个运行时问题，五个原语构成治理框架的核心分解；作者明确指出五部分分解与其实践恰好匹配并非偶然，强调这是实际代码库的反映而非纯粹的理论分类。

Abstract: Enterprise deployments of autonomous AI agents inherit a control model built for human users and long-lived services, and the fit fails in three specific ways: agent principals are ephemeral, appearing and vanishing faster than provisioning; their actions are selected by a model rather than programmed, so the set of things they may attempt is not known in advance; and the population is discovered rather than provisioned, because anyone who can call an API can create one. We argue that governing such agents is a runtime problem -- not a model-alignment problem and not a build-time problem -- and we derive five primitives from the questions that must be answered before an action takes effect and after it has: discovery, identity, governance, attestation, and supply chain. For each we state what fails if it is absent and why the others cannot structurally supply it. We describe an implementation in which an agent's action is mediated against policy before it takes effect, authorised against a per-tenant action vocabulary, and recorded in a hash-linked signed ledger a third party can verify with the vendor out of the loop. We report what the architecture costs: the enforcement point sits on the request's critical path, identity requires a sidecar per workload, and fail-closed mediation converts availability incidents into denial. We are explicit about implementation status: four primitives are built and running in private pilots, and the fifth is built as separate tooling and not yet integrated into the request path. We keep it in the set deliberately: a five-part decomposition that exactly matches what its authors happened to build is not a taxonomy but a description of a codebase.

</details>


### [272] [Accelerating Scientific Research with Gemini in the Real-World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26701)
*Samuel Schmidgall,Xiaokai Zhu,Marian Shaw,Lin Yang,Valentin Liévin,Jingyun Yang,Yuchen Zhuang,Tim Strother,Alex Bijamov,Min Woo Sun,Anil Palepu,Justin Chen,David Steiner,Jacqueline Shreibati,Wei-Hung Weng,Yilin Zhao,Xingjian Hu,Nicholas Zahn,Sadhya Garg,Julia Kirby,Yuxiang Gan,Jiaoli Li,Divy Thakkar,Shekoofeh Azizi,David Racz,Juraj Gottweis,Vivek Natarajan,Chenglin Wu,Tal Danino,Keran Rong,Haozhe Wang,Benoit Schillings,Yong Cheng,Quoc V. Le,Tao Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Co-Scientist系统的扩展版本，这是一个基于Gemini的多智能体系统，能够加速端到端的科学研究流程（假设生成、实验执行到论文撰写），并在材料科学、生物学和计算机科学三个领域进行了真实世界验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学AI系统大多停留在"in silico"假设生成阶段，缺乏与真实实验的结合。作者希望将AI科研助手从纯粹的假设生成器转变为能够执行实验、闭环驱动真实科学发现的系统。

Method: 基于Gemini 3 Deep Think构建多智能体系统Co-Scientist，集成了：1）与半自动化实验设备（化学气相沉积反应器）的接口；2）实验室闭环执行能力；3）可靠性模块（减少幻觉和抄袭）；4）多领域适配（材料科学、生物学、计算机科学）。

Result: 在材料科学中成功设计MXene前驱体路线并生长出2D层状材料；在几分钟内定制生长配方并成功单次生长MoS2、MoSe2和WS2单层半导体；在生物学中准确预测工程大肠杆菌的群集表型；在计算机科学中发现超越六个前沿模型的推理时扩展架构。双盲研究中的450份专家评审表明可靠性模块减少了幻觉和抄袭。

Conclusion: 研究表明多智能体科学AI系统能够实现闭环的真实世界科学发现，在材料合成、生物实验预测、模型架构发现等多个领域展示了实用价值，代表了向自动化科研迈进的实质性进展。

Abstract: We present an extension and comprehensive real-world validation of Co-Scientist, a Gemini-based multi-agent system designed to accelerate end-to-end scientific research across hypothesis generation, experimentation, and manuscript generation. Moving beyond in silico hypothesis generation, this specialized configuration transitions Co-Scientist into an execution-grounded research partner advancing closed-loop scientific workflows across materials science, biology, and computer science. In materials science, Co-Scientist interfaced with a semi-automated chemical vapor deposition reactor to design a safe precursor route for MXenes; experimental execution produced a lamellar 2D material sharing key structural similarities with the Ti3C2Tx MXene lattice, although further experiments are needed to confirm the atomic structure. Leveraging Gemini 3 Deep Think for rapid, lab-in-the-loop execution, it also tailored growth recipes to laboratory constraints in minutes, enabling single-attempt growth of monolayer MoS2, MoSe2, and WS2 semiconductors. In biology, Co-Scientist predicted emergent swarming phenotypes of engineered E. coli across inducer (IPTG) gradients from sparse imaging data, quantitatively matching unpublished wet-lab morphological measurements. In computer science, Co-Scientist autonomously discovered an inference-time scaling architecture that outperformed six frontier models on HealthBench (Hard and Professional) while reducing potential clinical harm under blinded physician evaluation. Finally, a double-blind study of end-to-end generated papers with 30 domain experts across 450 reviews demonstrates that Co-Scientist's reliability modules reduce hallucination and plagiarism while improving research safety. Together, these results demonstrate progress toward closed-loop multi-agent scientific AI systems capable of accelerating real-world scientific discovery.

</details>


### [273] [Style as a Confound: False Positives in AI Detection of Non-Native Academic Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26710)
*Hyeonchu Park,Gahye Jeong,Bugeun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用135,389对专业英文编辑前后文稿（2018-2025），在控制作者身份和内容的前提下，探讨专业编辑如何影响13种AI文本检测器的响应。研究发现不同检测器的误报率差异极大（0%-100%），同一编辑操作在不同检测器中导致AI分数升或降，且分数变化与编辑程度相关，表明专业编辑风格是AI检测器输出的关键混杂变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究虽已报告非母语英语写作的高误报率，但群体层面的比较混淆了作者身份与主题、领域和写作风格的差异，无法厘清检测器反映的究竟是AI作者身份还是"润色后的学术英语"这一语言学特征。专业编辑场景可在保持作者身份和内容不变的情况下仅改变语言形式，为检验该问题提供了理想条件。

Method: 收集135,389对来自专业英文编辑服务的文档对（2018-2025），包含非母语作者原稿及其母语编辑版本，控制内容与作者身份不变，测试13种AI文本检测器对编辑前后文档的响应差异，并分析分数变化与编辑程度的相关性。

Result: 13种AI检测器对人类书写文本的误报率从0.0%到100.0%差异极大；不同检测器对同一编辑操作的反应方向不一致（有的升高有的降低AI分数）；分数变化与编辑程度显著相关。

Conclusion: 专业编辑风格是AI检测器输出的关键混杂变量，但研究并未完全分离文本来源与语言风格的影响，这一发现对学术环境中AI检测工具的公平性和可靠性提出了严重关切。

Abstract: AI text detectors are increasingly employed in academic settings, but it remains unclear whether their outputs reflect AI authorship itself or broader linguistic features associated with polished academic English. Previous studies have reported high false-positive rates (FPRs) for non-native English writing, but population-level comparisons confound authorship with differences in topic, domain, and writing style. Professional editing provides a useful setting for examining this issue because it changes the linguistic form of manuscripts while preserving authorship and content. We examined 135,389 document pairs from a professional English editing service (2018-2025), comprising non-native manuscripts and their native-edited versions, to assess how editing affects detector responses controlling for content and authorship. For the 13 AI text detectors, FPRs for human-written texts varied widely, from 0.0% to 100.0%. Responses varied across detectors: the same edits increased AI scores in some detectors but decreased them in others. Notably, score changes correlated with the extent of editing. The findings identify professional editing style as a key confounding variable in AI detector outputs, rather than establishing a full separation of text origin from linguistic style, raising concerns about fairness and reliability in academic settings.

</details>


### [274] [Knowing When Not to Reuse: Conditional Experience Transfer in Autonomous LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26730)
*Tingyun Li,Wenfeng Feng,Weiqing Li,Abudukelimu Wuerkaixi,Guohua Liu,Yuewei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出边界校准干预迁移（BCIT）方法，解决自主后训练中条件经验迁移问题，即在不断变化的父模型上判断哪些过去的更新证据仍然有效，通过将效果绑定到源上下文、检查适用条件、否决硬冲突更新并在必要时进行有界训练试验，从而提高后训练效率和最终模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型适应不断变化的领域、工具和需求需要反复后训练，自主系统通过评估反馈自动提出和选择更新。但随着模型不断被训练，一个问题浮现：哪些过去的更新证据在父模型发生变化后仍然可行动？将过去的成功视为无上下文约束的许可会浪费计算，且若子模型被升级，还会退化后续训练轨迹。

Method: BCIT方法包含四个关键环节：(1) 将观察到的效果绑定到其来源上下文；(2) 检查可适用性条件；(3) 否决带有命名硬冲突的候选；(4) 在需要时通过有界训练试验获取当前状态证据。已完全训练的候选仍需通过共享采纳规则，且只有观察事件能扩展记忆。

Result: 在4B模型上针对金融推理、文本转SQL和函数调用三个场景的实验表明，候选更新在不同上下文中表现出异质的保留和迁移效应。在匹配候选、证据和计算量的条件下，BCIT授权更少的有害更新，并在等预算下获得更高的最终模型质量。

Conclusion: 本文支持将经验授权视为自主后训练中的独立问题，BCIT通过条件化经验迁移有效减少了有害更新并提升了训练质量。

Abstract: Large language models offer broad capabilities, but adapting them to evolving domains, tools, and requirements often entails repeated post-training. Autonomous systems automate parts of this process by proposing updates, training candidates, and using evaluation feedback to select subsequent proposals. As evidence accumulates, a central problem emerges: which past update evidence remains actionable after subsequent training has changed the parent model? An update's effect depends on its parent, data, and training stage. Treating past success as context-free permission can waste compute. If the resulting child is promoted, it can also degrade the subsequent training trajectory. We formulate this problem as conditional experience transfer and introduce Boundary-Calibrated Intervention Transfer (BCIT), a method that authorizes experience reuse before weight-changing training. BCIT binds an observed effect to its source context, checks applicability conditions, vetoes candidates with named hard conflicts, and obtains current-state evidence through a bounded training trial when needed. Fully trained candidates still face a shared adoption rule, and only observed events extend memory. On one 4B model adapted across finance reasoning, text-to-SQL, and function calling, candidate updates exhibit heterogeneous target and retention effects across the evaluated contexts. Under matched candidates, evidence, and compute, BCIT authorizes fewer harmful updates and attains higher equal-budget final-model quality than the evaluated alternatives. These results support treating experience authorization as a distinct problem in autonomous post-training.

</details>


### [275] [Graph-Guided Selective Unlearning for Language Models: Controlling Support Routes Beyond Forget Seeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26743)
*Waqas Khan,Tabinda Sarwar,Jingyue Cong,Xun Yi,Estrid He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GRAPHSU，一种图引导的控制器，通过构建加权支持-路由图来扩展机器遗忘的删除范围，而不仅限于显式给定的遗忘样本，从而更有效地防止私有数据通过改写、别名或邻近训练样本被恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业微调语言模型后，出于隐私、合同或合规要求可能需要删除特定数据。现有选择性遗忘方法仅处理显式标记的遗忘样本，但目标知识仍可通过改写、别名或邻近训练样本被恢复，删除范围不完整。

Method: GRAPHSU通过以下步骤实现扩展删除：1) 构建加权支持-路由图（support-route graph）；2) 通过图传播删除压力；3) 对高风险邻居施加分级（graded）遗忘强度，从而将删除范围从遗忘种子扩展到相关样本。

Result: 在TOFU和PISTOL基准上，使用GPT-2 Medium和Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct进行评估，GRAPHSU在所有删除设置下实现了最低的效用可行软泄漏，相比匹配的仅种子基线泄漏最多降低49.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 有效的企业级机器遗忘需要控制支持路由（support routes）而不仅仅是遗忘种子（forget seeds），GRAPHSU通过图引导的扩展删除策略证明了这一观点。

Abstract: Enterprises fine-tune language models on proprietary data that may later require removal due to privacy, contractual, or compliance obligations. Selective unlearning removes requested knowledge while preserving model utility, offering a practical alternative to full retraining, but existing methods treat the explicitly identified forget examples as the complete deletion scope. This is insufficient when target knowledge remains recoverable through paraphrases, aliases, or neighboring training examples. We propose GRAPHSU, a graph-guided controller that expands the deletion scope beyond forget seeds by constructing a weighted support-route graph, propagating deletion pressure through it, and applying graded forgetting strengths to high-risk neighbors. On the Task of Fictitious Unlearning (TOFU), a synthetic author-profile question-answering benchmark, and PISTOL, a structural-unlearning benchmark built around interconnected factual samples, with GPT-2 Medium and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, GRAPHSU achieves the lowest utility-feasible soft leakage across all deletion settings, reducing leakage by up to 49.5 percentage points over a matched seed-only baseline, demonstrating that effective enterprise unlearning requires controlling support routes, not just forget seeds.

</details>


### [276] [AgentFold: Closed-Loop Agentic Search for Protein Folding Model Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26747)
*Mingquan Liu,Jiangyu Chen,Hanqun Cao,Xujun Zhang,Pengsen Ma,Xiangru Tang,Shuting Jin,Zhuo Yang,Tianfan Fu,Fang Wu,Xiangxiang Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentFold是一个多智能体框架，利用LLM智能体通过可执行代码修改和计算验证，在蛋白质折叠领域自主改进科学机器学习系统，从ESMFold出发探索约80个模型变体，最终将lDDT指标提升7.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学LLM智能体在文献推理、工具使用和实验规划方面已展现潜力，但尚不清楚它们能否通过可执行代码更改和昂贵计算验证，自主改进大型、紧密耦合的科学机器学习系统。蛋白质折叠是此类挑战的典型代表，需要协调的架构修改、多目标评估和领域感知解释。

Method: AgentFold采用多智能体框架，将折叠模型开发表述为对可执行代码变体的闭环搜索。从ESMFold出发，智能体提出假设、实现并调试代码级修改、评估模型变体、分析实验结果，并将成功和失败的干预存储在结构化记忆中。使用MCTS风格策略在高分搜索分支间分配计算资源。

Result: 在2000多行代码的蛋白质折叠代码库上，AgentFold探索了约80个模型变体，消耗约5000 GPU小时和1.7亿LLM token。在匹配的计算预算下，AgentFold比独立的Codex提案提高最佳lDDT 7.5%，并优于随机搜索对照组。干预轨迹揭示了稳定的经验设计模式：早期软性可学习先验和门控精化有助于稳定收益，而直接几何扰动和几何条件反馈常常破坏训练稳定性。

Conclusion: AgentFold证明了LLM智能体能够自主改进大规模科学机器学习系统，不仅提升了模型性能，还产生了可复用的经验设计模式洞察，为科学自动化研究提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Scientific LLM agents have shown promise in literature reasoning, tool use, and experiment planning, but it remains unclear whether they can autonomously improve large, tightly coupled scientific machine-learning systems through executable code changes and computationally expensive validation. We study this question in protein folding, where progress requires coordinated architectural modifications, multi-objective evaluation, and domain-aware interpretation. We present AgentFold, a multi-agent framework that formulates folding-model development as a closed-loop search over executable code variants. Starting from ESMFold, AgentFold proposes hypotheses, implements and debugs code-level modifications, evaluates model variants, analyzes experimental outcomes, and stores both successful and failed interventions in structured memory. An MCTS-style policy allocates computational resources across high-scoring search branches. On an engineering-scale protein-folding codebase comprising more than 2,000 lines of code, AgentFold explores approximately 80 model variants using approximately 5,000 GPU-hours and 170 million LLM tokens. Under a matched computational budget, AgentFold improves the best lDDT by 7.5% over independent Codex proposals and outperforms a random-search control. Beyond model improvement, the resulting intervention traces reveal recurring empirical design patterns: stable gains tend to arise from early, soft, learnable priors and gated refinement, whereas direct geometric perturbations and geometry-conditioned feedback often destabilize training. The code and experimental resources are publicly available at https://github.com/lmqfly/AgentFold.

</details>


### [277] [Discovering Relationships in Data Lakes Using Large Language Models: An Industrial Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26750)
*Ahlame Diouan,Eric Ferey,Sabine Loudcher,Jérôme Darmont*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ColRel是一种两阶段方法，通过元数据和数据构建列嵌入，利用业务字典解释编码模式并生成自然语言描述，用于高效发现数据湖中的列关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据湖依赖元数据保持可用性，但元数据常常有限或信息量弱，尤其在ERP数据集中使用编码或缩写模式标签，导致列关系发现困难。

Method: 两阶段方法：第一阶段从摄入时的元数据和数据构建列嵌入；第二阶段在编码模式等困难场景下，利用业务字典解释列名并生成简短的自然语言描述来辅助列关系发现。

Result: 在公共基准和工业ERP数据集上的实验表明，ColRel在语义相关、弱信号场景下尤其有效。

Conclusion: ColRel通过结合元数据、数据和业务字典，有效解决了编码模式下数据湖列关系发现的痛点。

Abstract: Data lakes rely on metadata to remain usable, yet this meta data is often limited or weakly informative for column relationship discovery, especially in ERP-derived datasets with coded or abbreviated schema labels. We propose ColRel, a two-stage method that builds column embeddings from metadata and data available at ingestion time. In difficult cases, such as coded schemata, business dictionaries help better interpret column names and support the generation of short natural-language descriptions used in the second stage. Experiments on public benchmarks and an industrial ERP dataset show that ColRel is particularly effective in semantically related, weak-signal settings.

</details>


### [278] [DEEPCHART: How Far are LLMs from Faithful Data-Science Chart Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26757)
*Jiahui tang,Kuicai Dong,Dexun Li,Hongchao Gu,Haocheng Yu,Wei Han,Chen Zhang,Yong Liu,Hao Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DEEPCHART基准，包含1,482个来自真实科学论文、财务报告和生态报告的任务条件图表生成实例，将图表生成形式化为"提取-推理-可视化"流水线，并分阶段评估源数据提取、派生数据推理和图表渲染。实验发现视觉上合理的图表常隐藏数据级幻觉，仅靠更大的上下文窗口不足以解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM能够生成视觉上合理且符合指令的图表，但在长文本、嘈杂和多模态上下文中，数据级幻觉难以检测。作者希望量化这一差距，推动忠实图表生成的发展。

Method: 构建专家标注的DEEPCHART基准，包含1,482个图表生成实例，来自科学论文、财务文件和生态报告。将图表生成定义为Extract-Reason-Visualize三阶段流水线，对三个环节分别进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，最先进的模型生成的图表虽然视觉上合理，但经常隐藏数据级幻觉；提取和推理错误在真实长文本和多模态设置中非常普遍。

Conclusion: 仅扩大上下文窗口不足以保证图表生成的忠实性，可靠的证据提取和定量推理是渲染之前的关键前提。

Abstract: Faithful chart generation in real-world data-science workflows requires grounding visualizations in scattered evidence, computing chart-ready quantities, and rendering them accurately. Modern LLMs can produce visually plausible, instruction-compliant charts, yet data-level hallucinations remain difficult to detect in long, noisy, and multimodal contexts. To measure this gap, we introduce DEEPCHART, an expert-annotated benchmark of 1,482 task-conditioned chart-generation instances drawn from real-world scientific papers, financial filings, and ecosystem reports. DEEPCHART formulates chart generation as an Extract--Reason--Visualize pipeline and evaluates source-data extraction, derived-data reasoning, and chart rendering stage by stage. Experiments with state-of-the-art models show that visually plausible charts often conceal data-level hallucinations, with extraction and reasoning errors common in realistic long and multimodal settings. These findings suggest that larger context windows alone are insufficient; faithful chart generation also requires reliable evidence extraction and quantitative reasoning before rendering. Our benchmark and associated resources are available at https://github.com/tangdouer1005/DeepChart.

</details>


### [279] [Categorizer Automata for Discounted-Sum Payoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26763)
*Nathalie Bertrand,Pranav Ghorpade,Senthil Rajasekaran,Sasha Rubin,Moshe Vardi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"分类器自动机"（categorizer automaton），用于将无限奖励序列的折扣和分类到有限数量的容器中，其状态空间与容器数量呈线性关系，并应用于马尔可夫决策过程中的策略综合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将连续数据分类到离散容器是AI中的基本操作，现有比较器自动机仅处理两个容器且状态空间呈指数增长，需要更高效的方法。

Method: 构造分类器自动机，状态空间与容器数量呈线性关系（优于比较器自动机的指数空间），并将其应用于马尔可夫决策过程，用于综合最大化折扣和折现预期效用的策略。

Result: 对于分段常数效用函数，算法精确且在伪多项式时间内运行；对于分段Lipschitz效用函数（包括任何有界斜率且有限跳跃的效用），同样在伪多项式时间内运行并产生ε最优策略；同时证明该综合问题在分段常数效用下是PSPACE难的。

Conclusion: 分类器自动机为处理多容器分类问题提供了线性状态空间的解决方案，并在MDP策略综合中实现了高效且精确（或近似最优）的算法，但该问题本身是PSPACE难的。

Abstract: Categorizing continuous data into discrete bins is a fundamental operation in artificial intelligence. We introduce the categorizer automaton, a deterministic automaton that reads an infinite sequence of rewards and identifies which of finitely many bins contains its discounted sum. Categorizer automata generalize comparator automata, the special case of two bins, which have already proven useful in quantitative synthesis. Our main technical contribution is the construction of a categorizer automaton whose state space is linear in the number of bins, rather than exponential as obtained by a cross-product of comparator automata. We then apply categorizer automata to Markov decision processes, where they allow one to synthesize policies that maximize the expected utility of a discounted-sum payoff for utility functions that may be discontinuous. For piecewise-constant utility functions, the resulting algorithm is exact and runs in pseudo-polynomial time. For piecewise-Lipschitz utility functions, a class that includes any utility with bounded slope between finitely many jumps, it again runs in pseudo-polynomial time and yields an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy. We also show that the synthesis problem considered is PSPACE-hard already for piecewise-constant utilities.

</details>


### [280] [AI Control Scientist: LLM-driven Agentic System for Automated Control Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26780)
*Haiteng Wang,Weihao Li,Jing Zhang,Lei Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AI Control Scientist (AICS)，这是首个由大语言模型驱动的智能体系统，能够从语言设计需求中自动生成优化后的控制器，包含任务建模、控制器设计和参数调优三个智能体模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制系统设计依赖专家知识和大量手动参数调优，效率低且可扩展性差，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出三个LLM驱动的智能体模块：Task Modeling Agent将用户需求转化为工程约束，Controller Design Agent生成候选控制器结构和可执行代码，Parameter Tuning Agent在闭环性能标准下优化控制器参数。

Result: 实验表明该系统能自动生成多种代表性控制系统，在设计与成功率与优化效率上均优于现有自动化基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作有望将控制系统设计从人类驱动转变为智能体驱动，为模型预测控制等先进控制系统设计奠定基础。

Abstract: Control system design is critical for modern industry, such as chemical process temperature regulation and aero-engine control. However,traditional control design workflows rely heavily on expert knowledge and extensive manual parameter tuning, resulting in limited efficiency and scalability. To this end, this paper proposes AI Control Scientist (AICS), the first large language model (LLM)-driven agent capable of automatically generating optimized controller from language design requirements. Specifically, a Task Modeling Agent interprets user requirements to engineering constraints; a Controller Design Agent generate candidate controller structures and executable code; and a Parameter Tuning Agent refine controller parameters under closed-loop performance criteria. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed agentic system can automatically generate multiple representative control systems, outperforms existing automated baselines in both design success rate and optimization efficiency. This work has the potential to transform control system design from human-driven to agent-driven, paving the way for model predictive control and other advanced control systems design.

</details>


### [281] [SymbolLKG: Towards Verifiable Logical Reasoning via Logical Knowledge Graph and Symbolic Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26836)
*Haizhao Fan,Yuchi Xiong,Jize Wang,Xinping Guan,Xinyi Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号架构，将逻辑知识图谱与动态求解器路由相结合，以解决LLM在多步逻辑推理中缺乏严格验证机制的问题，实验表明其在逻辑推理基准上显著优于CoT和标准RAG方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽在自然语言理解上表现优异，但在严格的多步推理中常出现幻觉和不一致性问题；现有CoT缺乏验证机制，标准RAG无法捕捉逻辑任务中复杂的结构依赖关系。

Method: 提出一种神经符号架构：(1) 构建基于本体的逻辑知识图谱（LKG），将逻辑规则和约束作为一等地拓扑节点，显式建模文本中提取的依赖关系；(2) 设计逻辑路由器（Logic Router）动态分配任务到最优符号引擎；(3) 支持拓扑感知的混合检索机制。

Result: 在逻辑推理基准上，该框架显著优于最先进的提示方法和RAG基线，实现了更高的准确率和可验证的推理路径。

Conclusion: 将逻辑知识图谱与动态求解器路由结合的神经符号架构，能够有效提升LLM在逻辑推理任务中的准确性和可验证性，弥补了CoT和标准RAG的不足。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in natural language understanding, yet they struggle with strict multi-step reasoning, frequently suffering from hallucinations and inconsistency. Existing solutions like Chain-of-Thought (CoT) lack rigorous verification mechanisms, while standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) often misses the complex, structural dependencies inherent in logical tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose a Neuro-Symbolic architecture that integrates a Logical Knowledge Graph (LKG) with dynamic solver routing. Specifically, we introduce an ontology-based LKG that treats logical rules and constraints as first-class topological nodes, enabling explicit modeling of dependencies extracted from text. We further design a Logic Router to dynamically dispatch tasks to the optimal symbolic engine, which is supported by a topology-aware hybrid retrieval mechanism. Experimental results on logical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms state-of-the-art prompting and RAG baselines, delivering higher accuracy and verifiable reasoning paths.

</details>


### [282] [BekchiAI: Measuring, Observing, and Controlling LLM Agents in One Click](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26867)
*Mesut Toruk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BekchiAI，包含一个用于评估大型语言模型智能体技能（如工具调用序列、规划、安全判断等）的基准测试套件，以及一个用于监控和控制部署中智能体的Web平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以准确率为主的排行榜难以衡量LLM智能体的代理技能，如工具排序、依赖规划、不可信输入判断和生成论证的接地性，亟需更全面的评估体系。

Method: 构建BekchiAI-Benchmark，包含13个使用ReAct框架的工具型智能体，覆盖7类任务共2,057个确定性任务，使用验证器计算金标准答案（如SQL查询、DAG调度、闭式lambda），并定义超越准确率的行为指标（工具调用遵循度、URL幻觉、源匹配、token成本）；同时提供BekchiAI-Platform作为Web观测与控制层。

Result: 对四个模型（Qwen3.7-Max、gemma-4-31B-it、gemma4:26b、gpt-oss-120b）进行比较，发现关键差异体现在模型家族内部的性能分布而非总体上。

Conclusion: BekchiAI同时解决了智能体技能评估和部署监控两个问题，基准、评估工具和平台均已公开发布。

Abstract: Large language model agents reason, call tools, and act autonomously over many steps, but their agentic skills-correctly sequencing tools, planning under dependencies, judging untrusted inputs, and grounding generated arguments-are hard to measure with accuracy-only leaderboards.
